# ExpertAdvisor Development Instructions

## Project

- C++20 project built with Xcode 26.5 on macOS.
- Primary executable: LSTM_Release.
- Avoid Swift.
- Prefer existing C++ and Objective-C++ test patterns.
- PostgreSQL access uses libpqxx.
- Treat the database schema and persisted workflows as authoritative contracts.

## Build

From the repository root:

xcodebuild   -project ExpertAdvisor.xcodeproj   -scheme "LSTM Release"   -configuration Release   -derivedDataPath DerivedData/ExpertAdvisor   build

Do not run Clean unless explicitly requested.

## Tests

Run the smallest relevant tests first, then broader regression tests.

Do not launch tests or executables that may interfere with active scheduler,
training, inference, or analysis workers without checking first.

## Scheduler Safety

Before running LSTM_Release commands that could start work:

1. Inspect running LSTM_Release processes.
2. Check scheduler status.
3. Avoid interrupting active experiments unless explicitly requested.
4. Never alter production experiment rows merely to make tests pass.

## Architecture

Preserve the established layering:

CLI -> Service / Workflow -> Repository -> PostgreSQL

Do not bypass authoritative workflows with direct repository or SQL writes.

Preserve:

- deterministic identities
- transactional boundaries
- concurrency safety
- immutable persisted decisions and materializations
- explicit phase boundaries
- idempotent retry behavior

## Development Style

- Make the smallest change that satisfies the requested increment.
- Do not redesign adjacent phases.
- Do not implement future roadmap items.
- Prefer reuse of existing authoritative workflows.
- Add tests for concrete behavior and regressions.
- Update documentation when the public or operational contract changes.
- Treat compiler warnings as defects unless clearly justified.
- Compiler and tests are authoritative.
- Resolve compiler warnings, build errors, and failing tests before considering an implementation complete.

## Review Output

At completion, report:

1. Files changed.
2. Exact behavioral change.
3. Tests and build commands run.
4. Results.
5. Remaining risks or unverified assumptions.
6. `git status --short`
7. `git diff --stat`

## Current Development Status

Current branch:

Completed major phases:
- Phase 1: Checkpoint inference

- Phase 3: Continuation framework
- Phase 4: Recommendation campaigns
- Phase 5: Campaign execution
- Phase 6A: ...

- Phase 6C: ...

Always preserve compatibility with completed phases.
Do not redesign previously accepted architecture.

## Never

- Never run `xcodebuild clean` unless explicitly requested.
- Never modify production experiment data to simplify testing.
- Never bypass authoritative workflows.
- Never redesign completed phases during an incremental implementation.

-- Phase 6C: append-only administrative operator-review decisions for exact
-- persisted Phase 6B proposals. Approval here grants no activation,
-- execution, follow-up, queue, scheduler, worker, or experiment authority.

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS
experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event (
    recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id bigserial
        PRIMARY KEY CHECK (
            recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id > 0),
    review_contract_version integer NOT NULL CHECK (
        review_contract_version = 1),
    recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id bigint NOT NULL,
    proposal_contract_version integer NOT NULL CHECK (
        proposal_contract_version = 1),
    proposal_identity_canonical text COLLATE "C" NOT NULL CHECK (
        proposal_identity_canonical <> '' AND
        octet_length(proposal_identity_canonical) <= 1048576),
    proposal_identity_hash text NOT NULL CHECK (
        proposal_identity_hash ~ '^fnv1a64:[0-9a-f]{16}$'),
    review_decision text NOT NULL CHECK (
        review_decision IN ('approved', 'rejected')),
    reviewer_identity text COLLATE "C" NOT NULL CHECK (
        reviewer_identity ~
            '^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._@:/+\-]{0,127}$'),
    reason_text text COLLATE "C" NOT NULL CHECK (
        reason_text <> '' AND octet_length(reason_text) <= 4096),
    review_identity_canonical text COLLATE "C" NOT NULL CHECK (
        review_identity_canonical <> '' AND
        octet_length(review_identity_canonical) <= 1056768),
    review_identity_hash text NOT NULL CHECK (
        review_identity_hash ~ '^fnv1a64:[0-9a-f]{16}$'),
    created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_proposal_fk
        FOREIGN KEY (recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id)
        REFERENCES experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal(
            recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id)
        ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    CONSTRAINT recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_once_uidx
        UNIQUE (recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_identity_hash_idx
    ON experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event(
        review_identity_hash);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION
enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY INVOKER
AS $$
BEGIN
    PERFORM 1
    FROM experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal proposal
    WHERE proposal.recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id =
              NEW.recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id
      AND proposal.proposal_contract_version =
              NEW.proposal_contract_version
      AND proposal.proposal_identity_canonical =
              NEW.proposal_identity_canonical
      AND proposal.proposal_identity_hash = NEW.proposal_identity_hash;
    IF NOT FOUND THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review provenance mismatch'
            USING ERRCODE = '23514',
                  CONSTRAINT =
                      'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance_check';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DO $$
DECLARE review_schema text := current_schema();
BEGIN
    EXECUTE format(
        'ALTER FUNCTION %I.enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance() '
        'SET search_path TO pg_catalog, %I, pg_temp',
        review_schema, review_schema);
END $$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS
enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance_trigger
ON experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event;
CREATE TRIGGER
enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance_trigger
BEFORE INSERT ON
    experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event
FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION
    enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance();

COMMENT ON TABLE
experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event IS
    'Immutable Phase 6C administrative review only; never activation, execution, scheduling, experiment mutation, or follow-up authorization.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN
experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event.review_identity_canonical IS
    'Authoritative byte-exact Phase 6C review identity; the tagged hash is only an accelerator.';
COMMENT ON COLUMN
experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event.review_decision IS
    'Administrative approved or rejected outcome only; approval grants no action authority.';

REVOKE ALL PRIVILEGES ON
    experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event
    FROM PUBLIC, pqxx;
REVOKE ALL PRIVILEGES ON FUNCTION
    enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance()
    FROM PUBLIC, pqxx;

GRANT SELECT ON
    experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event
    TO pqxx;
GRANT INSERT (
    review_contract_version,
    recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id,
    proposal_contract_version,
    proposal_identity_canonical,
    proposal_identity_hash,
    review_decision,
    reviewer_identity,
    reason_text,
    review_identity_canonical,
    review_identity_hash)
ON experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event
TO pqxx;

DO $$
DECLARE sequence_name text;
BEGIN
    sequence_name := pg_get_serial_sequence(
        'experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event',
        'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id');
    EXECUTE format(
        'REVOKE ALL PRIVILEGES ON SEQUENCE %s FROM PUBLIC', sequence_name);
    EXECUTE format(
        'REVOKE ALL PRIVILEGES ON SEQUENCE %s FROM pqxx', sequence_name);
    EXECUTE format(
        'GRANT USAGE ON SEQUENCE %s TO pqxx', sequence_name);
END $$;
DO $migration_test$
DECLARE
    review_table text := format(
        '%I.experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event',
        current_schema());
    proposal_table text := format(
        '%I.experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal',
        current_schema());
    review_sequence text;
    review_function regprocedure;
BEGIN
    IF to_regclass(review_table) IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review-event table missing';
    END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = to_regclass(review_table)
          AND conname =
              'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_proposal_fk'
          AND contype = 'f'
          AND confrelid = to_regclass(proposal_table)
          AND confdeltype = 'r') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review restrictive FK missing';
    END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = to_regclass(review_table)
          AND conname =
              'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_once_uidx'
          AND contype = 'u') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'one-review-per-proposal uniqueness missing';
    END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_indexes
        WHERE schemaname = current_schema()
          AND tablename =
              'experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event'
          AND indexname =
              'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_identity_hash_idx')
    THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review hash index missing';
    END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_trigger
        WHERE tgrelid = to_regclass(review_table)
          AND NOT tgisinternal
          AND tgname =
              'enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance_trigger')
    THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review provenance trigger missing';
    END IF;
    IF (
        SELECT count(*) FROM pg_attribute attribute
        JOIN pg_collation coll ON coll.oid = attribute.attcollation
        WHERE attribute.attrelid = to_regclass(review_table)
          AND attribute.attname IN (
              'proposal_identity_canonical',
              'review_identity_canonical')
          AND coll.collname = 'C') <> 2 THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'review authoritative canonicals are not byte-exact';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns
        WHERE table_schema = current_schema()
          AND table_name =
              'experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event'
          AND column_name IN (
              'activated', 'execution_authorized', 'follow_up_authorized',
              'queued', 'scheduled', 'scheduler_started',
              'scheduler_signaled', 'workers_started',
              'experiments_created', 'experiments_modified',
              'campaign_success_declared', 'superseded', 'expired')) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'review-event table contains forbidden authority state';
    END IF;

    IF NOT has_table_privilege('pqxx', review_table, 'SELECT') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review runtime SELECT missing';
    END IF;
    IF has_table_privilege('pqxx', review_table, 'INSERT') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review table INSERT too broad';
    END IF;
    IF NOT has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', review_table, 'review_contract_version', 'INSERT') OR
       NOT has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', review_table, 'review_decision', 'INSERT') OR
       NOT has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', review_table, 'review_identity_canonical', 'INSERT') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review payload INSERT missing';
    END IF;
    IF has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', review_table,
            'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id',
            'INSERT') OR
       has_column_privilege(
            'pqxx', review_table, 'created_at', 'INSERT') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review generated columns writable';
    END IF;
    IF has_table_privilege('pqxx', review_table, 'UPDATE') OR
       has_table_privilege('pqxx', review_table, 'DELETE') OR
       has_table_privilege('pqxx', review_table, 'TRUNCATE') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review mutation privilege present';
    END IF;
    IF has_function_privilege(
           'pqxx',
           'enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance()',
           'EXECUTE') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review trigger privilege present';
    END IF;

    review_function :=
        'enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance()'::regprocedure;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_proc function
        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
            function.proacl, acldefault('f', function.proowner))) privilege
        WHERE function.oid = review_function
          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review trigger PUBLIC privilege present';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_proc function
        WHERE function.oid = review_function
          AND (function.prosecdef OR function.proconfig IS DISTINCT FROM
              ARRAY[format('search_path=pg_catalog, %s, pg_temp',
                  current_schema())])) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review trigger execution context unsafe';
    END IF;

    review_sequence := pg_get_serial_sequence(
        review_table,
        'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id');
    IF NOT has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', review_sequence, 'USAGE') OR
       has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', review_sequence, 'SELECT') OR
       has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', review_sequence, 'UPDATE') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review sequence privilege incorrect';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_class relation
        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
            relation.relacl, acldefault('S', relation.relowner))) privilege
        WHERE relation.oid = review_sequence::regclass
          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review sequence PUBLIC privilege present';
    END IF;
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_class relation
        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
            relation.relacl, acldefault('r', relation.relowner))) privilege
        WHERE relation.oid = to_regclass(review_table)
          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review PUBLIC privilege present';
    END IF;
END
$migration_test$;

#include "ExperimentRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewPresentation.hpp"

#include <iomanip>
#include <locale>
#include <ostream>
#include <sstream>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace EA::ExperimentRecommendation
{
namespace
{

std::string ReviewMachineText(const std::string& value)
{
    std::ostringstream escaped;
    escaped.imbue(std::locale::classic());
    escaped << std::uppercase << std::hex;
    for (const unsigned char character : value)
    {
        const bool alphanumeric =
            (character >= 'a' && character <= 'z') ||
            (character >= 'A' && character <= 'Z') ||
            (character >= '0' && character <= '9');
        const bool safe = alphanumeric || character == '-' ||
            character == '_' || character == '.' || character == ':' ||
            character == '/' || character == ';';
        if (safe) escaped << static_cast<char>(character);
        else
            escaped << '%' << std::setw(2) << std::setfill('0')
                    << static_cast<unsigned int>(character);
    }
    return escaped.str() == "NULL" ? "%4E%55%4C%4C" : escaped.str();
}

void PrintSafety(std::ostream& output)
{
    output << "read_only=true,persisted=true,administrative_review=true,"
              "activated=false,execution_authorized=false,"
              "follow_up_authorized=false,queued=false,scheduled=false,"
              "scheduler_started=false,scheduler_signaled=false,"
              "workers_started=false,experiments_created=false,"
              "experiments_modified=false,campaign_success_declared=false";
}

void UseReadOnlyRepeatableRead(pqxx::read_transaction& transaction)
{
    transaction.exec(
        "SET TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ, READ ONLY;");
}

void PrintFailure(
    std::ostream& errors,
    const std::string& recordType,
    const std::string& reason)
{
    errors << recordType << ",reason="
           << ReviewMachineText(reason) << ',';
    PrintSafety(errors);
    errors << '\n';
}

} // namespace

void WriteRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
    std::ostream& output,
    const PersistedRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview& persisted)
{
    const auto& review = persisted.review;
    output
        << "RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_FOLLOW_UP_PROPOSAL_REVIEW"
        << ",review_event_id=" << std::to_string(persisted.reviewEventId)
        << ",follow_up_proposal_id="
        << std::to_string(review.followUpProposalId)
        << ",proposal_identity_hash="
        << ReviewMachineText(review.proposalIdentityHash)
        << ",review_contract_version="
        << std::to_string(review.identity.contractVersion)
        << ",review_identity_hash="
        << ReviewMachineText(review.identity.hash)
        << ",decision="
        << RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecisionText(
               review.decision)
        << ",reviewer="
        << ReviewMachineText(review.reviewerIdentity)
        << ",reason=" << ReviewMachineText(review.reasonText)
        << ",created_at="
        << ReviewMachineText(persisted.createdAt) << ',';
    PrintSafety(output);
    output << '\n';
}

int RunShowRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
    const std::string& connectionString,
    long long reviewEventId,
    std::ostream& output,
    std::ostream& errors)
{
    try
    {
        pqxx::connection connection{connectionString};
        pqxx::read_transaction transaction{connection};
        UseReadOnlyRepeatableRead(transaction);
        if (!RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewSchemaExists(
                transaction))
            throw std::runtime_error(
                "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_schema_required");
        const auto persisted =
            FindRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
                transaction, reviewEventId);
        if (!persisted)
        {
            errors << "RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_FOLLOW_UP_PROPOSAL_REVIEW_NOT_FOUND"
                   << ",review_event_id=" << std::to_string(reviewEventId)
                   << ',';
            PrintSafety(errors);
            errors << '\n';
            return 1;
        }
        WriteRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
            output, *persisted);
        return 0;
    }
    catch (const std::invalid_argument& error)
    {
        PrintFailure(errors,
            "RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_FOLLOW_UP_PROPOSAL_REVIEW_INVALID",
            error.what());
        return 1;
    }
    catch (const std::exception& error)
    {
        PrintFailure(errors,
            "RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_FOLLOW_UP_PROPOSAL_REVIEW_FAILED",
            error.what());
        return 2;
    }
}

int RunListRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviews(
    const std::string& connectionString,
    int limit,
    std::ostream& output,
    std::ostream& errors)
{
    try
    {
        pqxx::connection connection{connectionString};
        pqxx::read_transaction transaction{connection};
        UseReadOnlyRepeatableRead(transaction);
        if (!RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewSchemaExists(
                transaction))
            throw std::runtime_error(
                "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_schema_required");
        const auto reviews =
            ListRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviews(
                transaction, limit);
        for (const auto& review : reviews)
            WriteRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
                output, review);
        return 0;
    }
    catch (const std::invalid_argument& error)
    {
        PrintFailure(errors,
            "RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_FOLLOW_UP_PROPOSAL_REVIEW_LIST_INVALID",
            error.what());
        return 1;
    }
    catch (const std::exception& error)
    {
        PrintFailure(errors,
            "RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_FOLLOW_UP_PROPOSAL_REVIEW_LIST_FAILED",
            error.what());
        return 2;
    }
}

PHASE 6C INTENT

Phase 6C records one immutable administrative operator-review decision over one exact persisted Phase 6B follow-up proposal.

Supported decisions are exactly:

- `approved`
- `rejected`

An approved Phase 6C review means only:

- an operator administratively approved the exact persisted advisory proposal for possible consideration by a later explicitly authorized phase.

It does not mean:

- activated;
- execution authorized;
- follow-up authorized;
- queued;
- scheduled;
- scheduler work;
- scheduler signaled;
- worker launch;
- experiment creation;
- experiment modification;
- campaign success;
- profitability;
- statistical validation.

A rejected review must be equally immutable and auditable.

No Phase 6A or Phase 6B row may be mutated.

    86	       NOT has_column_privilege(
    87	            'pqxx', review_table, 'review_identity_canonical', 'INSERT') THEN
    88	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review payload INSERT missing';
    89	    END IF;
    90	    IF has_column_privilege(
    91	            'pqxx', review_table,
    92	            'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id',
    93	            'INSERT') OR
    94	       has_column_privilege(
    95	            'pqxx', review_table, 'created_at', 'INSERT') THEN
    96	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review generated columns writable';
    97	    END IF;
    98	    IF has_table_privilege('pqxx', review_table, 'UPDATE') OR
    99	       has_table_privilege('pqxx', review_table, 'DELETE') OR
   100	       has_table_privilege('pqxx', review_table, 'TRUNCATE') THEN
   101	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review mutation privilege present';
   102	    END IF;
   103	    IF has_function_privilege(
   104	           'pqxx',
   105	           'enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance()',
   106	           'EXECUTE') THEN
   107	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review trigger privilege present';
   108	    END IF;
   109	
   110	    review_function :=
   111	        'enforce_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_provenance()'::regprocedure;
   112	    IF EXISTS (
   113	        SELECT 1 FROM pg_proc function
   114	        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
   115	            function.proacl, acldefault('f', function.proowner))) privilege
   116	        WHERE function.oid = review_function
   117	          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
   118	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review trigger PUBLIC privilege present';
   119	    END IF;
   120	    IF EXISTS (
   121	        SELECT 1 FROM pg_proc function
   122	        WHERE function.oid = review_function
   123	          AND (function.prosecdef OR function.proconfig IS DISTINCT FROM
   124	              ARRAY[format('search_path=pg_catalog, %s, pg_temp',
   125	                  current_schema())])) THEN
   126	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review trigger execution context unsafe';
   127	    END IF;
   128	
   129	    review_sequence := pg_get_serial_sequence(
   130	        review_table,
   131	        'recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id');
   132	    IF NOT has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', review_sequence, 'USAGE') OR
   133	       has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', review_sequence, 'SELECT') OR
   134	       has_sequence_privilege('pqxx', review_sequence, 'UPDATE') THEN
   135	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review sequence privilege incorrect';
   136	    END IF;
   137	    IF EXISTS (
   138	        SELECT 1 FROM pg_class relation
   139	        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
   140	            relation.relacl, acldefault('S', relation.relowner))) privilege
   141	        WHERE relation.oid = review_sequence::regclass
   142	          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
   143	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review sequence PUBLIC privilege present';
   144	    END IF;
   145	    IF EXISTS (
   146	        SELECT 1 FROM pg_class relation
   147	        CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(coalesce(
   148	            relation.relacl, acldefault('r', relation.relowner))) privilege
   149	        WHERE relation.oid = to_regclass(review_table)
   150	          AND privilege.grantee = 0) THEN
   151	        RAISE EXCEPTION 'follow-up proposal review PUBLIC privilege present';
   152	    END IF;
   153	END
   154	$migration_test$;

    69	std::string EnvironmentOr(const char* name, const char* fallback)
    70	{
    71	    const char* value = std::getenv(name);

    75	std::string ReadFile(const std::string& path)
    76	{
    77	    std::ifstream input{path};
    78	    if (!input) throw std::runtime_error("unable_to_read_" + path);
    79	    return {std::istreambuf_iterator<char>{input},
    80	        std::istreambuf_iterator<char>{}};
    81	}

    83	bool SafeDisposableDatabaseName(const std::string& value)
    84	{
    85	    if (value.empty()) return false;
    86	    std::string lower = value;
    87	    std::transform(lower.begin(), lower.end(), lower.begin(),
    88	        [](unsigned char character)
    89	        { return static_cast<char>(std::tolower(character)); });
    90	    if (lower == "lstm") return false;
    91	    return std::all_of(value.begin(), value.end(),
    92	        [](unsigned char character)
    93	        { return std::isalnum(character) || character == '_' ||
    94	                 character == '-'; });
    95	}

    97	bool PermissionDenied(const pqxx::sql_error& error)
    98	{
    99	    return error.sqlstate() == "42501" ||
   100	        std::string(error.what()).find("permission denied") !=
   101	            std::string::npos;
   102	}
   103	
   104	void SetSearchPath(
   105	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
   106	    const std::string& schema)
   107	{
   108	    transaction.exec(
   109	        "SET LOCAL search_path TO " + transaction.quote_name(schema) + ";");
   110	}
   111	
   112	Context ComparisonContext(int seed)
   113	{
   114	    return {"eurusd", 12, 0.01, 24,
   115	        "phase6c-label-" + std::to_string(seed), "2026-06-01",
   116	        "2026-06-30"};
   117	}
   118	
   119	Metric Numeric(std::string identity, double value)
   120	{
   121	    return {std::move(identity), value, MetricSupport::NumericDelta};
   122	}
   123	
   124	MemberEvidence FavorableMember(int seed)
   125	{
   126	    MemberIdentity identity(1, 1000 + seed, 2000 + seed, 3000 + seed,
   127	        4000 + seed, 5000 + seed, 6000 + seed);
   128	    return {identity, Lifecycle::Succeeded,
   129	        SourceEvidence(identity.sourceExperimentId, 7000 + seed,
   130	            8000 + seed, ComparisonContext(seed),
   131	            MetricCollection({Numeric("inference_accuracy", 0.70),
   132	                Numeric("leader_score", 0.80)})),
   133	        ResultEvidence(identity.expectedExperimentId, 9000 + seed,
   134	            {ResultIdentity{"inference", 10000 + seed}},
   135	            ComparisonContext(seed),
   136	            MetricCollection({Numeric("inference_accuracy", 0.75),
   137	                Numeric("leader_score", 0.85)}))};
   138	}
   139	
   140	Proposal BuildProposal(int seed)
   141	{
   142	    const std::string campaignCanonical =
   143	        "phase6c-campaign-" + std::to_string(seed);
   144	    CampaignIdentity campaign(100 + seed, campaignCanonical,
   145	        RecommendationCanonicalHash(campaignCanonical));
   146	    const std::string materializationCanonical =
   147	        "phase6c-materialization-" + std::to_string(seed);
   148	    MaterializationIdentity materialization(200 + seed,
   149	        campaign.campaignApprovalId, campaign.identityHash, 1, 1,
   150	        materializationCanonical,
   151	        RecommendationCanonicalHash(materializationCanonical));
   152	    const Assessment assessment =
   153	        BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(campaign,
   154	            materialization, "2026-07-21 12:00:00+00",
   155	            {FavorableMember(seed)});
   156	    const auto policy = BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy(PolicyInput{});
   157	    const auto policyDecision = ApplyRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy(
   158	        policy, assessment);
   159	    return BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposal(
   160	        assessment, policyDecision);
   161	}
   162	
   163	long long InsertAndPersistProposal(
   164	    pqxx::connection& owner,
   165	    const std::string& schema,
   166	    const std::string& runtimeConnectionString,
   167	    const Proposal& proposal)
   168	{
   169	    {
   170	        pqxx::work setup{owner};
   171	        SetSearchPath(setup, schema);
   172	        setup.exec(
   173	            "INSERT INTO experiment_recommendation_campaign_approval "
   174	            "VALUES($1,$2,$3);",
   175	            pqxx::params{proposal.campaignIdentity.campaignApprovalId,
   176	                proposal.campaignIdentity.identityCanonical,
   177	                proposal.campaignIdentity.identityHash});
   178	        setup.exec(
   179	            "INSERT INTO experiment_recommendation_campaign_materialization "
   180	            "VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6,$7);",
   181	            pqxx::params{
   182	                proposal.materializationIdentity.materializationId,
   183	                proposal.materializationIdentity.campaignApprovalId,
   184	                proposal.materializationIdentity.contractVersion,
   185	                proposal.materializationIdentity.campaignIdentityHash,
   186	                proposal.materializationIdentity.memberCount,
   187	                proposal.materializationIdentity.identityCanonical,
   188	                proposal.materializationIdentity.identityHash});
   189	        const auto& member = proposal.members.front();
   190	        setup.exec(
   191	            "INSERT INTO experiment_recommendation_campaign_"
   192	            "materialization_member VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6,$7);",
   193	            pqxx::params{member.identity.materializationMemberId,
   194	                proposal.materializationIdentity.materializationId,
   195	                member.identity.memberOrdinal,
   196	                member.identity.rankingMemberId,
   197	                member.identity.recommendationId,
   198	                member.identity.sourceExperimentId,
   199	                member.identity.proposalId});
   200	        setup.commit();
   201	    }
   202	    pqxx::connection runtime{runtimeConnectionString};
   203	    pqxx::work transaction{runtime};
   204	    const auto result = PersistRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposal(
   205	        transaction, proposal);
   206	    assert(result.outcome ==
   207	        RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalPersistOutcome::recorded);
   208	    transaction.commit();
   209	    return result.persisted.followUpProposalId;
   210	}
   211	
   212	Review MakeReview(
   213	    long long proposalId,
   214	    const Proposal& proposal,
   215	    Decision decision,
   216	    std::string reviewer,
   217	    std::string reason)
   218	{
   219	    return BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(proposalId,
   220	        proposal, decision, std::move(reviewer), std::move(reason));
   221	}
   222	
   223	std::string TableSnapshot(
   224	    pqxx::connection& owner,
   225	    const std::string& schema,
   226	    const std::string& table,
   227	    const std::string& orderColumn)
   228	{
   229	    pqxx::read_transaction transaction{owner};
   230	    SetSearchPath(transaction, schema);
   231	    return transaction.exec(
   232	        "SELECT coalesce(string_agg(row_to_json(snapshot)::text,'|' ORDER BY " +
   233	        orderColumn + "),'') FROM (SELECT * FROM " +
   234	        transaction.quote_name(table) + ") snapshot;")
   235	        .one_row()[0].as<std::string>();
   236	}
   237	
   238	std::pair<long long, bool> ReviewSequenceState(
   239	    pqxx::connection& owner,
   240	    const std::string& schema)
   241	{
   242	    pqxx::read_transaction transaction{owner};
   243	    SetSearchPath(transaction, schema);
   244	    const std::string sequence = transaction.exec(
   245	        "SELECT relation.relname FROM pg_class relation WHERE relation.oid="
   246	        "pg_get_serial_sequence('experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_"
   247	        "up_proposal_review_event','recommendation_campaign_follow_up_"
   248	        "proposal_review_event_id')::regclass;")
   249	                                     .one_row()[0].as<std::string>();
   250	    const auto row = transaction.exec(
   251	        "SELECT last_value,is_called FROM " +
   252	        transaction.quote_name(sequence) + ";")
   253	                         .one_row();
   254	    return {row[0].as<long long>(), row[1].as<bool>()};
   255	}
   256	
   257	template <typename Corruption>
   258	void AssertMalformedReloadRejected(
   259	    pqxx::connection& owner,
   260	    const std::string& schema,
   261	    long long reviewEventId,
   262	    Corruption&& corruption,
   263	    const std::string& expectedFragment)
   264	{
   265	    bool rejected = false;
   266	    {
   267	        pqxx::work transaction{owner};
   268	        SetSearchPath(transaction, schema);
   269	        corruption(transaction);
   270	        try
   271	        {
   272	            (void)FindRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
   273	                transaction, reviewEventId);
   274	        }
   275	        catch (const std::runtime_error& error)
   276	        {
   277	            const std::string message = error.what();
   278	            rejected = message.starts_with(
   279	                           "invalid_persisted_recommendation_campaign_"
   280	                           "follow_up_proposal_review:") &&
   281	                message.find(expectedFragment) != std::string::npos;
   282	        }
   283	    }
   284	    assert(rejected);
   285	}
   286	
   287	template <typename Function>
   288	void AssertInvalidArgument(
   289	    Function&& function,
   290	    const std::string& expected)
   291	{
   292	    bool rejected = false;
   293	    try
   294	    {
   295	        function();
   296	    }
   297	    catch (const std::invalid_argument& error)
   298	    {
   299	        rejected = error.what() == expected;
   300	    }
   301	    assert(rejected);
   302	}

   306	int main()
   307	{
   308	    const char* testDatabase = std::getenv("LSTM_TEST_DB_NAME");
   309	    if (testDatabase == nullptr || *testDatabase == '\0')
   310	    {
   311	        std::cerr << "LSTM_TEST_DB_NAME_required\n";
   312	        return 2;
   313	    }
   314	    const std::string database = testDatabase;
   315	    if (!SafeDisposableDatabaseName(database))
   316	    {
   317	        std::cerr << "active_LSTM_database_forbidden\n";
   318	        return 2;
   319	    }
   320	    const std::string host = EnvironmentOr("LSTM_TEST_DB_HOST", "127.0.0.1");
   321	    const std::string port = EnvironmentOr("LSTM_TEST_DB_PORT", "5432");
   322	    const std::string ownerUser = EnvironmentOr(
   323	        "LSTM_TEST_DB_ADMIN_USER", EnvironmentOr("USER", "vjp").c_str());
   324	    const std::string schema =
   325	        "phase6c_follow_up_review_" + std::to_string(getpid());
   326	    const std::string ownerConnectionString =
   327	        "host=" + host + " port=" + port + " user=" + ownerUser +
   328	        " dbname=" + database;
   329	    const std::string runtimeConnectionString =
   330	        "host=" + host + " port=" + port + " user=pqxx dbname=" + database +
   331	        " options='-c search_path=" + schema + "'";
   332	    pqxx::connection owner{ownerConnectionString};
   333	
   334	    try
   335	    {
   336	        {
   337	            pqxx::work setup{owner};
   338	            setup.exec("CREATE SCHEMA " + setup.quote_name(schema) + ";");
   339	            SetSearchPath(setup, schema);
   340	            setup.exec(R"SQL(
   341	CREATE TABLE experiment_recommendation_campaign_approval(
   342	    recommendation_campaign_approval_id bigint PRIMARY KEY,
   343	    approval_identity_canonical text NOT NULL,
   344	    approval_identity_hash text NOT NULL);
   345	CREATE TABLE experiment_recommendation_campaign_materialization(
   346	    recommendation_campaign_materialization_id bigint PRIMARY KEY,
   347	    recommendation_campaign_approval_id bigint NOT NULL,
   348	    materialization_contract_version integer NOT NULL,
   349	    approval_identity_hash text NOT NULL,
   350	    selected_member_count integer NOT NULL,
   351	    materialization_identity_canonical text NOT NULL,
   352	    materialization_identity_hash text NOT NULL);
   353	CREATE TABLE experiment_recommendation_campaign_materialization_member(
   354	    recommendation_campaign_materialization_member_id bigint PRIMARY KEY,
   355	    recommendation_campaign_materialization_id bigint NOT NULL,
   356	    member_ordinal integer NOT NULL,
   357	    recommendation_ranking_member_id bigint NOT NULL,
   358	    recommendation_id bigint NOT NULL,
   359	    source_experiment_id bigint NOT NULL,
   360	    recommendation_conversion_proposal_id bigint NOT NULL);
   361	CREATE TABLE experiment(placeholder text PRIMARY KEY);
   362	CREATE TABLE experiment_scheduler(placeholder text PRIMARY KEY);
   363	INSERT INTO experiment VALUES('unchanged');
   364	INSERT INTO experiment_scheduler VALUES('unchanged');
   365	)SQL");
   366	            setup.exec("GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA " + setup.quote_name(schema) +
   367	                " TO pqxx; GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA " +
   368	                setup.quote_name(schema) + " TO pqxx;");
   369	            setup.exec(ReadFile(
   370	                "Database/migrations/042_experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal.sql"));
   371	            const std::string migration = ReadFile(
   372	                "Database/migrations/043_experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review.sql");
   373	            setup.exec(migration);
   374	            setup.exec(migration);
   375	            setup.exec(ReadFile(
   376	                "Tests/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewMigrationTests.sql"));
   377	            setup.commit();
   378	        }
   379	        {
   380	            pqxx::nontransaction session{owner};
   381	            session.exec(
   382	                "SET search_path TO " + session.quote_name(schema) + ";");
   383	        }
   384	
   385	        const Proposal proposal1 = BuildProposal(1);
   386	        const Proposal proposal2 = BuildProposal(2);
   387	        const Proposal proposal3 = BuildProposal(3);
   388	        const Proposal proposal4 = BuildProposal(4);

   390	            owner, schema, runtimeConnectionString, proposal1);

   392	            owner, schema, runtimeConnectionString, proposal2);
   393	        const long long proposalId3 = InsertAndPersistProposal(
   394	            owner, schema, runtimeConnectionString, proposal3);
   395	        const long long proposalId4 = InsertAndPersistProposal(
   396	            owner, schema, runtimeConnectionString, proposal4);

   399	            "experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal",
   400	            "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id");
   401	        const std::string memberSnapshotBefore = TableSnapshot(owner, schema,
   402	            "experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_member",
   403	            "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_member_id");

   411	            Decision::approved, "operator.one", "Approved administratively.");
   412	
   413	        PersistOutcome identicalFirst = PersistOutcome::recorded;
   414	        PersistOutcome identicalSecond = PersistOutcome::recorded;
   415	        long long identicalFirstId = 0;

   417	        std::exception_ptr identicalFirstError;

   429	                    std::this_thread::yield();
   430	                const auto result =

   931	                cleanup.quote_name(schema) + " CASCADE;");
   932	            cleanup.commit();
   933	        }
   934	        catch (...)
   935	        {
   936	        }
   937	        throw;
   938	    }

## Executive summary

Phase 6C is ready for correction staging. Independent review found and corrected one medium-severity presentation defect and two low-severity verification/documentation gaps. No authority-boundary, persistence, concurrency, schema, provenance, or scheduler defect remains.

The repository state differed from the prompt: Phase 6C was already committed at `2e1fc63d77af04949090a28f67be03b4fbea7be1`, and the helper files already matched HEAD rather than being staged changes. I reviewed the exact Phase 6C commit delta (`HEAD^..HEAD`) plus the four unstaged corrections.

## Findings and corrections

1. Medium — locale-dependent machine output

   Numeric IDs were streamed through the caller’s locale, allowing grouped output such as `review_event_id=5,678` to corrupt the comma-delimited format.

   Corrected in [ExperimentRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewPresentation.cpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewPresentation.cpp:69>) by rendering integers with locale-independent decimal conversion.

2. Low — migration verification gaps

   Tests did not explicitly verify:

   - no PUBLIC privilege on the sequence;
   - no PUBLIC execution privilege on the trigger function;
   - `SECURITY INVOKER`;
   - the hardened `pg_catalog, <schema>, pg_temp` search path.

   Added assertions in [ExperimentRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewMigrationTests.sql](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Tests/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewMigrationTests.sql:107>).

3. Low — presentation test gap

   Added hostile-locale and delimiter/UTF-8 escaping coverage in [ExperimentRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewRepositoryTests.cpp](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Tests/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewRepositoryTests.cpp:829>).

4. Low — documentation precision

   Clarified that reason validation rejects ASCII-whitespace-only input, rather than claiming full Unicode whitespace classification. Documented locale-independent numeric rendering and percent escaping in [Phase6CRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview.rst](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/docs/Phase6CRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview.rst:45>).

## Contract, identity, and validation assessment

The domain contract is immutable and non-assignable, with exactly `approved` and `rejected` decisions. It binds the persisted proposal ID, proposal version/canonical/hash, reviewer, reason, decision, full review canonical, and review hash.

The canonical grammar is deterministic and locale-independent. Arbitrary valid proposal, reviewer, and reason bytes are unambiguously represented using byte-length prefixes. Generated event ID and timestamp remain outside identity. Tagged FNV-1a hashes are treated only as accelerators; canonical equality is authoritative.

Validation order and explicit failure reasons are deterministic. Unsupported versions, invalid IDs, canonical/hash disagreement, invalid enum values, reviewer/reason constraints, persisted corruption, provenance mismatch, and injected authority state fail closed.

## Migration and repository assessment

Migration 043 is additive and repeatable and creates one append-only review-event table with:

- restrictive `ON DELETE RESTRICT` Phase 6B foreign key;
- one-event-per-proposal uniqueness;
- exact `approved`/`rejected` constraint;
- `C` collation on authoritative canonicals;
- bounded canonical/hash fields;
- generated ID and timestamp;
- exact-provenance insert trigger;
- hardened invoker function search path;
- runtime `SELECT`, payload-column `INSERT`, and sequence `USAGE` only.

Actual runtime insertion succeeded. Runtime has no broad insert, generated-column insert, update, delete, truncate, sequence select/update, or trigger execution privilege.

Persistence validates the immutable value and exact Phase 6B proposal before insertion. The advisory lock is derived from the exact persisted proposal ID, not the proposal identity hash. A theoretical 64-bit advisory-key collision can only conservatively serialize unrelated IDs; exact-ID lookup and uniqueness prevent semantic conflation.

Concurrent identical attempts converge on `recorded` plus `existing_identical`. Concurrent conflicting reviews produce one recorded event and one deterministic conflict. No update, supersede, or delete path exists.

## Presentation and authority assessment

Show/list use explicit repeatable-read, read-only transactions. Verification confirmed:

- deterministic not-found and limit errors;
- descending event-ID ordering;
- all required negative authority fields;
- locale-independent and delimiter-safe output;
- no sequence advancement;
- no advisory or tuple locks;
- no writes.

No Phase 6C CLI integration or scheduler consumer exists. No scheduler source changed. Approval remains administrative evidence only and cannot activate, authorize, queue, schedule, signal, launch workers, create or modify experiments, declare success, assert profitability, or imply statistical validation.

    15	bool IsAsciiAlphanumeric(unsigned char character)
    16	{
    17	    return (character >= 'a' && character <= 'z') ||
    18	        (character >= 'A' && character <= 'Z') ||
    19	        (character >= '0' && character <= '9');
    20	}
    21	
    22	bool IsValidReviewerIdentity(const std::string& value)
    23	{
    24	    if (value.empty() ||
    25	        value.size() >
    26	            kMaximumRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewerIdentityBytes ||
    27	        !IsAsciiAlphanumeric(static_cast<unsigned char>(value.front())))
    28	        return false;
    29	    for (const unsigned char character : value)
    30	    {
    31	        if (!IsAsciiAlphanumeric(character) && character != '.' &&
    32	            character != '_' && character != '@' && character != ':' &&
    33	            character != '/' && character != '+' && character != '-')
    34	            return false;
    35	    }
    36	    return true;
    37	}
    38	
    39	bool IsValidUtf8(const std::string& value)
    40	{
    41	    const auto* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(value.data());
    42	    std::size_t index = 0;
    43	    while (index < value.size())
    44	    {
    45	        const unsigned char first = bytes[index];
    46	        if (first <= 0x7fU)
    47	        {
    48	            ++index;
    49	            continue;
    50	        }
    51	        std::size_t continuationCount = 0;
    52	        unsigned int codePoint = 0;
    53	        if (first >= 0xc2U && first <= 0xdfU)
    54	        {
    55	            continuationCount = 1;
    56	            codePoint = first & 0x1fU;
    57	        }
    58	        else if (first >= 0xe0U && first <= 0xefU)
    59	        {
    60	            continuationCount = 2;
    61	            codePoint = first & 0x0fU;
    62	        }
    63	        else if (first >= 0xf0U && first <= 0xf4U)
    64	        {
    65	            continuationCount = 3;
    66	            codePoint = first & 0x07U;
    67	        }
    68	        else return false;
    69	        if (index + continuationCount >= value.size()) return false;
    70	        for (std::size_t offset = 1; offset <= continuationCount; ++offset)
    71	        {
    72	            const unsigned char next = bytes[index + offset];
    73	            if ((next & 0xc0U) != 0x80U) return false;
    74	            codePoint = (codePoint << 6U) | (next & 0x3fU);
    75	        }
    76	        if ((continuationCount == 2 && codePoint < 0x800U) ||
    77	            (continuationCount == 3 && codePoint < 0x10000U) ||
    78	            (codePoint >= 0xd800U && codePoint <= 0xdfffU) ||
    79	            codePoint > 0x10ffffU)
    80	            return false;
    81	        index += continuationCount + 1;
    82	    }
    83	    return true;
    84	}
    85	
    86	bool IsValidReasonText(const std::string& value)
    87	{
    88	    if (value.empty() ||
    89	        value.size() >
    90	            kMaximumRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewReasonTextBytes ||
    91	        !IsValidUtf8(value))
    92	        return false;
    93	    bool containsNonWhitespace = false;
    94	    for (const unsigned char character : value)
    95	    {
    96	        if (character == 0U || character == 0x7fU ||
    97	            (character < 0x20U && character != '\t' && character != '\n' &&
    98	                character != '\r'))
    99	            return false;
   100	        if (character >= 0x80U ||
   101	            (character != ' ' && character != '\t' && character != '\n' &&
   102	                character != '\r'))
   103	            containsNonWhitespace = true;
   104	    }
   105	    return containsNonWhitespace;
   106	}
   107	
   108	std::string LengthPrefixed(const std::string& value)
   109	{
   110	    return std::to_string(value.size()) + ":" + value;
   111	}
   112	
   113	std::string ReviewCanonicalText(
   114	    long long followUpProposalId,
   115	    int proposalContractVersion,
   116	    const std::string& proposalCanonicalText,
   117	    const std::string& proposalIdentityHash,
   118	    RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecision decision,
   119	    const std::string& reviewerIdentity,
   120	    const std::string& reasonText)
   121	{
   122	    std::ostringstream output;
   123	    output.imbue(std::locale::classic());
   124	    output
   125	        << "experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_v1"
   126	        << ";review_contract_version="
   127	        << kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewContractVersion
   128	        << ";read_only=true"
   129	           ";database_free=true"
   130	           ";persistent=false"
   131	           ";administrative_review=true"
   132	           ";activated=false"
   133	           ";execution_authorized=false"
   134	           ";follow_up_authorized=false"
   135	           ";queued=false"
   136	           ";scheduled=false"
   137	           ";scheduler_started=false"
   138	           ";scheduler_signaled=false"
   139	           ";workers_started=false"
   140	           ";experiments_created=false"
   141	           ";experiments_modified=false"
   142	           ";campaign_success_declared=false"
   143	        << ";follow_up_proposal_id=" << followUpProposalId
   144	        << ";proposal_contract_version=" << proposalContractVersion
   145	        << ";proposal_canonical=" << LengthPrefixed(proposalCanonicalText)
   146	        << ";proposal_identity_hash="
   147	        << LengthPrefixed(proposalIdentityHash)
   148	        << ";decision=" << RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecisionText(
   149	               decision)
   150	        << ";reviewer=" << LengthPrefixed(reviewerIdentity)
   151	        << ";reason=" << LengthPrefixed(reasonText);
   152	    return output.str();
   153	}

   156	
   157	struct RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewBuilder
   158	{
   159	    static RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview Build(
   160	        int reviewContractVersion,
   161	        long long followUpProposalId,
   162	        int proposalContractVersion,
   163	        const std::string& proposalCanonicalText,
   164	        const std::string& proposalIdentityHash,
   165	        RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecision decision,
   166	        std::string reviewerIdentity,
   167	        std::string reasonText)
   168	    {
   169	        if (reviewContractVersion !=
   170	            kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewContractVersion)
   171	            throw std::invalid_argument("review_contract_unsupported");
   172	        if (followUpProposalId <= 0)
   173	            throw std::invalid_argument("persisted_proposal_id_invalid");
   174	        if (proposalContractVersion !=
   175	            kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalContractVersion)
   176	            throw std::invalid_argument("proposal_contract_unsupported");
   177	        if (proposalCanonicalText.empty() ||
   178	            proposalCanonicalText.size() >
   179	                kMaximumRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalCanonicalTextBytes ||
   180	            proposalCanonicalText.find('\0') != std::string::npos ||
   181	            proposalIdentityHash !=
   182	                RecommendationCanonicalHash(proposalCanonicalText))
   183	            throw std::invalid_argument("proposal_identity_invalid");
   184	        (void)RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecisionText(
   185	            decision);
   186	        if (!IsValidReviewerIdentity(reviewerIdentity))
   187	            throw std::invalid_argument("reviewer_identity_invalid");
   188	        if (!IsValidReasonText(reasonText))
   189	            throw std::invalid_argument("review_reason_invalid");
   190	
   191	        std::string canonicalText = ReviewCanonicalText(followUpProposalId,
   192	            proposalContractVersion, proposalCanonicalText,
   193	            proposalIdentityHash, decision, reviewerIdentity, reasonText);
   194	        if (canonicalText.size() >
   195	            kMaximumRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewCanonicalTextBytes)
   196	            throw std::invalid_argument("review_canonical_size_exceeded");
   197	        RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewIdentity identity(
   198	            reviewContractVersion, canonicalText,
   199	            RecommendationCanonicalHash(canonicalText));
   200	        return RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
   201	            std::move(identity), followUpProposalId,
   202	            proposalContractVersion, proposalCanonicalText,
   203	            proposalIdentityHash, decision, std::move(reviewerIdentity),
   204	            std::move(reasonText));
   205	    }
   206	};
   207	
   208	RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewIdentity::
   209	    RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewIdentity(
   210	        int contractVersionValue,
   211	        std::string canonicalTextValue,
   212	        std::string hashValue)
   213	    : contractVersion(contractVersionValue),
   214	      canonicalText(std::move(canonicalTextValue)),
   215	      hash(std::move(hashValue))
   216	{
   217	}
   218	
   219	RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview::
   220	    RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
   221	        RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewIdentity identityValue,
   222	        long long followUpProposalIdValue,
   223	        int proposalContractVersionValue,
   224	        std::string proposalCanonicalTextValue,
   225	        std::string proposalIdentityHashValue,
   226	        RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecision decisionValue,
   227	        std::string reviewerIdentityValue,
   228	        std::string reasonTextValue)
   229	    : identity(std::move(identityValue)),
   230	      followUpProposalId(followUpProposalIdValue),
   231	      proposalContractVersion(proposalContractVersionValue),
   232	      proposalCanonicalText(std::move(proposalCanonicalTextValue)),
   233	      proposalIdentityHash(std::move(proposalIdentityHashValue)),
   234	      decision(decisionValue),
   235	      reviewerIdentity(std::move(reviewerIdentityValue)),
   236	      reasonText(std::move(reasonTextValue))
   237	{
   238	}
   239	
   240	RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview
   241	BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
   242	    int reviewContractVersion,
   243	    long long followUpProposalId,
   244	    int proposalContractVersion,
   245	    const std::string& proposalCanonicalText,
   246	    const std::string& proposalIdentityHash,
   247	    RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecision decision,
   248	    std::string reviewerIdentity,
   249	    std::string reasonText)
   250	{
   251	    return RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewBuilder::Build(
   252	        reviewContractVersion, followUpProposalId, proposalContractVersion,
   253	        proposalCanonicalText, proposalIdentityHash, decision,
   254	        std::move(reviewerIdentity), std::move(reasonText));
   255	}
   256	
   257	RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview
   258	BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
   259	    long long followUpProposalId,
   260	    const RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposal& proposal,
   261	    RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecision decision,
   262	    std::string reviewerIdentity,
   263	    std::string reasonText)
   264	{
   265	    return BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
   266	        kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewContractVersion,
   267	        followUpProposalId, proposal.identity.contractVersion,
   268	        proposal.identity.canonicalText, proposal.identity.hash, decision,
   269	        std::move(reviewerIdentity), std::move(reasonText));
   270	}
   271	
   272	void ValidateRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
   273	    const RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview& review)
   274	{
   275	    const auto rebuilt = BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
   276	        review.identity.contractVersion, review.followUpProposalId,
   277	        review.proposalContractVersion, review.proposalCanonicalText,
   278	        review.proposalIdentityHash, review.decision,
   279	        review.reviewerIdentity, review.reasonText);
   280	    if (rebuilt != review)
   281	        throw std::invalid_argument("review_identity_mismatch");
   282	}
   283	
   284	std::string RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecisionText(
   285	    RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecision decision)
   286	{
   287	    switch (decision)
   288	    {
   289	        case RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecision::approved:
   290	            return "approved";
   291	        case RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecision::rejected:
   292	            return "rejected";
   293	    }
   294	    throw std::invalid_argument("review_decision_invalid");
   295	}
   296	
   297	RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecision
   298	RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecisionFromText(
   299	    const std::string& text)
   300	{
   301	    if (text == "approved")
   302	        return RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecision::approved;
   303	    if (text == "rejected")
   304	        return RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecision::rejected;
   305	    throw std::invalid_argument("review_decision_invalid");
   306	}

    43	class GroupedNumberPunctuation final : public std::numpunct<char>
    44	{
    45	protected:
    46	    char do_thousands_sep() const override { return ','; }
    47	    std::string do_grouping() const override { return "\1"; }

    50	template <typename Function>
    51	void AssertReason(Function&& function, const std::string& expected)
    52	{
    53	    bool threw = false;
    54	    try
    55	    {
    56	        function();
    57	    }
    58	    catch (const std::exception& error)
    59	    {
    60	        threw = true;
    61	        assert(error.what() == expected);
    62	    }
    63	    assert(threw);
    64	}
    65	
    66	Review Build(
    67	    Decision decision = Decision::approved,
    68	    long long proposalId = 41,
    69	    std::string proposalCanonical = "phase6a-proposal",
    70	    std::string reviewer = "operator@example.test",
    71	    std::string reason = "Evidence reviewed manually.")
    72	{
    73	    const std::string proposalHash =
    74	        RecommendationCanonicalHash(proposalCanonical);
    75	    return BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
    76	        kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewContractVersion,
    77	        proposalId,
    78	        kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalContractVersion,
    79	        proposalCanonical, proposalHash, decision, std::move(reviewer),
    80	        std::move(reason));
    81	}
    82	
    83	} // namespace
    84	
    85	int main()
    86	{
    87	    const Review approved = Build();
    88	    const Review rejected = Build(Decision::rejected);
    89	    assert(approved.decision == Decision::approved);
    90	    assert(rejected.decision == Decision::rejected);
    91	    assert(approved.followUpProposalId == 41);
    92	    assert(approved.proposalContractVersion ==
    93	        kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalContractVersion);
    94	    assert(approved.identity.contractVersion ==
    95	        kRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewContractVersion);
    96	    assert(approved.identity.hash ==
    97	        RecommendationCanonicalHash(approved.identity.canonicalText));
    98	    ValidateRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(approved);
    99	    ValidateRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(rejected);
   100	
   101	    const std::string expectedCanonical =
   102	        "experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_v1"
   103	        ";review_contract_version=1"
   104	        ";read_only=true"
   105	        ";database_free=true"
   106	        ";persistent=false"
   107	        ";administrative_review=true"
   108	        ";activated=false"
   109	        ";execution_authorized=false"
   110	        ";follow_up_authorized=false"
   111	        ";queued=false"
   112	        ";scheduled=false"
   113	        ";scheduler_started=false"
   114	        ";scheduler_signaled=false"
   115	        ";workers_started=false"
   116	        ";experiments_created=false"
   117	        ";experiments_modified=false"
   118	        ";campaign_success_declared=false"
   119	        ";follow_up_proposal_id=41"
   120	        ";proposal_contract_version=1"
   121	        ";proposal_canonical=16:phase6a-proposal"
   122	        ";proposal_identity_hash=24:" +
   123	        approved.proposalIdentityHash +
   124	        ";decision=approved"
   125	        ";reviewer=21:operator@example.test"
   126	        ";reason=27:Evidence reviewed manually.";
   127	    assert(approved.identity.canonicalText == expectedCanonical);
   128	    assert(Build() == approved);
   129	
   130	    const std::locale originalLocale = std::locale();
   131	    std::locale::global(
   132	        std::locale(originalLocale, new GroupedNumberPunctuation));
   133	    const Review localized = Build();
   134	    std::locale::global(originalLocale);
   135	    assert(localized == approved);
   136	
   137	    assert(Build(Decision::approved, 42).identity != approved.identity);
   138	    assert(Build(Decision::approved, 41, "phase6a-proposal-2").identity !=
   139	        approved.identity);
   140	    assert(Build(Decision::approved, 41, "phase6a-proposal",
   141	               "second-reviewer")
   142	            .identity != approved.identity);
   143	    assert(Build(Decision::approved, 41, "phase6a-proposal",
   144	               "operator@example.test", "A different reason.")
   145	            .identity != approved.identity);
   146	    assert(rejected.identity != approved.identity);
   147	
   148	    assert(RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecisionText(
   149	               Decision::approved) == "approved");
   150	    assert(RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecisionText(
   151	               Decision::rejected) == "rejected");
   152	    assert(RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecisionFromText(
   153	               "approved") == Decision::approved);
   154	    assert(RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecisionFromText(
   155	               "rejected") == Decision::rejected);
   156	
   157	    AssertReason([&]
   158	    {
   159	        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(2, 41, 1,
   160	            approved.proposalCanonicalText, approved.proposalIdentityHash,
   161	            Decision::approved, "reviewer", "reason");
   162	    }, "review_contract_unsupported");
   163	    AssertReason([&]
   164	    {
   165	        (void)Build(Decision::approved, 0);
   166	    }, "persisted_proposal_id_invalid");
   167	    AssertReason([&]
   168	    {
   169	        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(1, 41, 2,
   170	            approved.proposalCanonicalText, approved.proposalIdentityHash,
   171	            Decision::approved, "reviewer", "reason");
   172	    }, "proposal_contract_unsupported");
   173	    AssertReason([&]
   174	    {
   175	        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(1, 41, 1,
   176	            approved.proposalCanonicalText, "fnv1a64:0000000000000000",
   177	            Decision::approved, "reviewer", "reason");
   178	    }, "proposal_identity_invalid");
   179	    AssertReason([&]
   180	    {
   181	        const std::string nulCanonical("proposal\0identity", 17);
   182	        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(1, 41, 1,
   183	            nulCanonical, RecommendationCanonicalHash(nulCanonical),
   184	            Decision::approved, "reviewer", "reason");
   185	    }, "proposal_identity_invalid");
   186	    AssertReason([&]
   187	    {
   188	        (void)Build(static_cast<Decision>(99));
   189	    }, "review_decision_invalid");
   190	    AssertReason([&]
   191	    {
   192	        (void)Build(Decision::approved, 41, "phase6a-proposal", "", "reason");
   193	    }, "reviewer_identity_invalid");
   194	    AssertReason([&]
   195	    {
   196	        (void)Build(Decision::approved, 41, "phase6a-proposal", "bad identity",
   197	            "reason");
   198	    }, "reviewer_identity_invalid");
   199	    AssertReason([&]
   200	    {
   201	        (void)Build(Decision::approved, 41, "phase6a-proposal", "reviewer",
   202	            "   \t\n");
   203	    }, "review_reason_invalid");
   204	    AssertReason([&]
   205	    {
   206	        (void)Build(Decision::approved, 41, "phase6a-proposal", "reviewer",
   207	            std::string("bad\0reason", 10));
   208	    }, "review_reason_invalid");
   209	    AssertReason([&]
   210	    {
   211	        (void)Build(Decision::approved, 41, "phase6a-proposal", "reviewer",
   212	            std::string("\xc3\x28", 2));
   213	    }, "review_reason_invalid");
   214	    AssertReason([&]
   215	    {
   216	        (void)RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecisionFromText(
   217	            "pending");
   218	    }, "review_decision_invalid");
   219	
   220	    // No Phase 6C canonical can encode positive action authority.
   221	    for (const std::string forbidden : {
   222	             "activated=true", "execution_authorized=true",
   223	             "follow_up_authorized=true", "queued=true", "scheduled=true",
   224	             "scheduler_started=true", "scheduler_signaled=true",
   225	             "workers_started=true", "experiments_created=true",
   226	             "experiments_modified=true",
   227	             "campaign_success_declared=true"})
   228	        assert(approved.identity.canonicalText.find(forbidden) ==
   229	            std::string::npos);
   230	
   231	    return 0;
   232	}

codex

I’m probing two test-quality details before execution: whether the migration assertion binds the actual trigger function unambiguously, and whether the hostile-locale assertions are exact enough to reject malformed records.

    14	
    15	std::string ReviewColumns()
    16	{
    17	    return
    18	        "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id,"
    19	        "review_contract_version,"
    20	        "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id,"
    21	        "proposal_contract_version,proposal_identity_canonical,"
    22	        "proposal_identity_hash,review_decision,reviewer_identity,"
    23	        "reason_text,review_identity_canonical,review_identity_hash,"
    24	        "created_at::text AS created_at";
    25	}
    26	
    27	void ValidateProposalBinding(
    28	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
    29	    const RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview& review)
    30	{
    31	    const auto persistedProposal =
    32	        FindRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposal(
    33	            transaction, review.followUpProposalId);
    34	    if (!persistedProposal)
    35	        throw std::runtime_error(
    36	            "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_proposal_not_found");
    37	    const auto& proposal = persistedProposal->proposal;
    38	    if (proposal.identity.contractVersion != review.proposalContractVersion ||
    39	        proposal.identity.canonicalText != review.proposalCanonicalText ||
    40	        proposal.identity.hash != review.proposalIdentityHash)
    41	        throw std::runtime_error(
    42	            "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_proposal_mismatch");
    43	}
    44	
    45	Persisted MapReview(
    46	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
    47	    const pqxx::row& row)
    48	{
    49	    try
    50	    {
    51	        const long long reviewEventId = row[
    52	            "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event_id"]
    53	                                                .as<long long>();
    54	        const std::string createdAt =
    55	            row["created_at"].as<std::string>();
    56	        if (reviewEventId <= 0 || createdAt.empty())
    57	            throw std::runtime_error("persisted_review_metadata_invalid");
    58	        auto review = BuildRecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReview(
    59	            row["review_contract_version"].as<int>(),
    60	            row["recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id"]
    61	                .as<long long>(),
    62	            row["proposal_contract_version"].as<int>(),
    63	            row["proposal_identity_canonical"].as<std::string>(),
    64	            row["proposal_identity_hash"].as<std::string>(),
    65	            RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewDecisionFromText(
    66	                row["review_decision"].as<std::string>()),
    67	            row["reviewer_identity"].as<std::string>(),
    68	            row["reason_text"].as<std::string>());
    69	        if (review.identity.canonicalText !=
    70	                row["review_identity_canonical"].as<std::string>() ||
    71	            review.identity.hash !=
    72	                row["review_identity_hash"].as<std::string>())
    73	            throw std::runtime_error("review_identity_mismatch");
    74	        ValidateProposalBinding(transaction, review);
    75	        return Persisted(
    76	            reviewEventId, std::move(review), createdAt);
    77	    }
    78	    catch (const std::exception& error)
    79	    {
    80	        throw std::runtime_error(
    81	            "invalid_persisted_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review:" +
    82	            std::string(error.what()));
    83	    }
    84	}
    85	
    86	std::optional<Persisted> FindByProposalId(
    87	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
    88	    long long followUpProposalId)
    89	{
    90	    const pqxx::result rows = transaction.exec(
    91	        "SELECT " + ReviewColumns() + " FROM "
    92	        "experiment_recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_event "
    93	        "WHERE recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_id=$1 LIMIT 2;",
    94	        pqxx::params{followUpProposalId});
    95	    if (rows.empty()) return std::nullopt;
    96	    if (rows.size() != 1)
    97	        throw std::runtime_error(
    98	            "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_duplicate");
    99	    return MapReview(transaction, rows.one_row());
   100	}

   124	std::string RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewPersistOutcomeText(
   125	    RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewPersistOutcome outcome)
   126	{
   127	    switch (outcome)
   128	    {
   129	        case RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewPersistOutcome::
   130	                recorded:
   131	            return "recorded";
   132	        case RecommendationCampaignFollowUpProposalReviewPersistOutcome::
   133	                existingIdentical:
   134	            return "existing_identical";
   135	    }
   136	    throw std::invalid_argument(
   137	        "recommendation_campaign_follow_up_proposal_review_persist_outcome_invalid");
   138	}
